import type { IncidentStatus } from "../agent/state";
import { ACTION_IDS, type ActionId, type Category, type Findings } from "../diagnosis/categories";

/**
 * Action catalog (TDD §7.6). The model and the rules only *name* actions; this decides which one is shown as
 * an approval card, and nothing runs until a human approves it (FR-20, FR-24).
 */
export const ACTION_TITLES: Record<ActionId, string> = {
  restore_demo_service: "Restore the demo service to healthy",
  add_to_watchlist: "Add this endpoint to the watchlist",
  recheck_burst: "Re-check every minute for 15 minutes",
  generate_postmortem: "Write a postmortem",
};

export type ProposeContext = {
  target: string;
  workspaceId: string;
  demoBaseUrl: string;
  category: Category;
  status: IncidentStatus;
};

/** True when the target is this workspace's own demo page: someone else's demo, or any other site, is off limits. */
export function isOwnDemo(target: string, workspaceId: string, demoBaseUrl: string): boolean {
  if (!URL.canParse(target) || !URL.canParse(demoBaseUrl)) return false;
  const t = new URL(target);
  return t.origin === new URL(demoBaseUrl).origin && t.pathname.startsWith(`/w/${workspaceId}/`);
}

export function proposable(action: ActionId, ctx: ProposeContext): boolean {
  // A new card only fits these statuses (TDD §10): mitigated and resolved have no edge to awaiting_approval.
  if (ctx.status !== "investigating" && ctx.status !== "awaiting_approval") return false;
  switch (action) {
    case "restore_demo_service":
      return ctx.category !== "healthy" && isOwnDemo(ctx.target, ctx.workspaceId, ctx.demoBaseUrl);
    // Switched on by their WPs: add_to_watchlist (WP-18), generate_postmortem (WP-22), recheck_burst (WP-24).
    case "add_to_watchlist":
    case "recheck_burst":
    case "generate_postmortem":
      return false;
  }
}

/**
 * The model's actions first, then the rule actions, so a broken demo always gets "Restore demo service"
 * even when the model leaves it out (user decision, WP-12). The first proposable one wins (one per run).
 */
export function pickAction(findings: Pick<Findings, "category" | "actions">, ctx: ProposeContext): ActionId | null {
  const rules: ActionId[] = findings.category === "healthy" ? [] : ["restore_demo_service"];
  const candidates = [...new Set([...findings.actions, ...rules])].filter((a): a is ActionId => (ACTION_IDS as readonly string[]).includes(a));
  return candidates.find((a) => proposable(a, ctx)) ?? null;
}

export type ActionOutcome = { ok: boolean; text: string };

/** Runs an approved action. Idempotent: a retried Workflow step may call it again. */
export async function execute(action: ActionId, env: Pick<Env, "DEMO">, workspaceId: string): Promise<ActionOutcome> {
  switch (action) {
    case "restore_demo_service":
      await env.DEMO.setModes(workspaceId, ["healthy"]);
      return { ok: true, text: "The demo service is back in healthy mode." };
    default:
      return { ok: false, text: `${ACTION_TITLES[action]} isn't available yet.` };
  }
}
----
import type { IncidentStatus } from "./state";

/** Post-action monitor (D11, TDD §8.4): quick checks on agent schedule ticks, never inside a Workflow. */
export const MONITOR_TICK_S = 15;
export const MONITOR_MAX_TICKS = 10;
/** Passes in a row that resolve a mitigated incident. The verify check after an action counts as the first. */
export const MONITOR_PASSES = 3;

export type MonitorState = { ticksLeft: number; passes: number };
export type TickResult = {
  monitor: MonitorState | null;
  /** The status to move to, if it changes. */
  to: IncidentStatus | null;
};

/**
 * One tick, pure. A pass counts up (and recovers an investigating incident to mitigated); 3 in a row resolve
 * it. A fail resets the count and reopens a mitigated incident. The monitor ends when resolved or out of ticks.
 */
export function tick(state: MonitorState, status: IncidentStatus, pass: boolean): TickResult {
  const ticksLeft = state.ticksLeft - 1;
  const passes = pass ? state.passes + 1 : 0;
  let to: IncidentStatus | null = null;
  if (pass && status === "investigating") to = "mitigated";
  else if (pass && status === "mitigated" && passes >= MONITOR_PASSES) to = "resolved";
  else if (!pass && status === "mitigated") to = "investigating";
  const done = to === "resolved" || ticksLeft <= 0 || status === "resolved";
  return { monitor: done ? null : { ticksLeft, passes }, to };
}

- **Local check:** all five modes, driven through `/api/demo/mode` and a real Workflow, gave the expected category (slow → `high_latency`, errors → `origin_5xx`, redirect_loop → `redirect_loop`, bad_cache → `cache_misconfiguration`, healthy → `healthy`). ### 7.6 Action catalog (`actions/catalog.ts`)

| ID | Args | Proposable when | Execute | Verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `restore_demo_service` | `{}` | Target host = demo host, path starts with `/w/<incident.workspaceId>/`, category ≠ `healthy` | `env.DEMO.setModes(ws, ["healthy"])` (clears every mode) | `quickCheck` pass, then `afterAction` |
| `add_to_watchlist` | `{intervalSec: 300}` | Not already watched and under caps | Insert into `watches` | None; outcome goes on the timeline |
| `recheck_burst` | `{}` | No monitor active | Agent `monitor(kind:"burst")` | Ticks |
| `generate_postmortem` | `{}` | status = `resolved` | `generatePostmortem()` | None |

The Workflow drops any action that isn't proposable. At most one action per run.

*As built (WP-12), `worker/actions/catalog.ts`:*
- `proposable(action, ctx)` is pure. `restore_demo_service` needs:
----
- **`expired` overrides an `approved` card that never ran.** This happens when an approve races the timeout.
- **The status leaves `awaiting_approval` only when no card is pending any more.**
- **Duplicates (D13):** the same action as a pending card is a duplicate, gets a `proposal_deduped` timeline event, and that run doesn't wait.
- **Resolving by hand** closes pending cards as rejected ("resolved by hand") and stops the monitor.
- **Errors from `approveWorkflow` / `rejectWorkflow`** are logged and never thrown.

### 8.4 Monitoring (D11), in `agent/monitor.ts`

1. `afterAction(pass)` sets passes = 1, status = `mitigated`, and schedules `monitorTick` every 15 s (10 ticks at most).
2. Each tick runs `quickCheck`. A pass adds one to the count (3 passes → `resolved`). A fail resets the count (`mitigated` → `investigating`).
3. When the ticks run out, the incident stays `mitigated`.
4. Bursts work the same way at 60 s × 15.
5. For watch incidents, `reportWatchCheck` from cron feeds the same counter. In that case a pass while `investigating` moves to `mitigated` (recovered without action).

*As built (WP-12):*
- **The pure reducer.** `monitor.ts` `tick(state, status, pass)` holds the logic, with `MONITOR_TICK_S` 15, `MONITOR_MAX_TICKS` 10 and `MONITOR_PASSES` 3.
- **One tick at a time.** Each tick is a one-shot `this.schedule(15, "monitorTick")`, chained to the next, and its `schedule_id` is stored. `resolve()` cancels it.
- **Timeline.** Every tick writes a `monitor_tick` event, and running out of ticks writes `monitor_ended`.
- **How long it takes.** The verify check counts as pass 1, so a restored demo resolves about 30 s after Approve.

### 8.5 Watch (cron, D12)

1. `scheduled()` runs `SELECT * FROM watches WHERE next_run <= now AND expires_at > now ORDER BY next_run LIMIT 6`.
2. For each watch, it runs `quickCheck` with a subrequest counter (stopping before 50) and updates `next_run`, `last_status` and `consecutive_passes`.
3. If `open_incident_id` is set, it calls `agent.reportWatchCheck(pass)`.
4. If the check fails and no incident is open, it calls `WATCH_WORKFLOW.create({id: "watch-<id>-<minute>"})`. Duplicate IDs are ignored.
5. Expired watches are deleted.

`WatchWorkflow`:
1. `step.sleep("30 seconds")`, then a re-check, done twice (2 of 3 failures confirm).
2. If not confirmed, end.
3. Otherwise `step.do("open")` creates an incident ID, calls `agent.init({origin: "watch", watchId})` and sets `watches.open_incident_id`. `init` starts the diagnosis.
4. `step.do("notify")` creates `NOTIFY_WORKFLOW` with `notify-<id>-opened`.

The Agent creates a NotifyWorkflow for watch incidents when they transition to mitigated or resolved (D18). When an incident resolves, the watch's `open_incident_id` is cleared.

### 8.6 Follow-up

`sendMessage(text)` checks the role, then `activeRunId` (409 if set), then the quota, then `startRun(text, "followup")`. The context includes earlier evidence digests, and the model may call 0 tools before `submit_findings`. A hostname in the text that's outside the origin pin gets a system message with a "New incident for X" button.

### 8.7 Voice and postmortem

**Voice:** `MediaRecorder` with `audio/webm;codecs=opus` at 24 kbps and a 30 s auto-stop. The Worker handles `POST /api/voice`:
- checks the 160 KB size cap and the limit (from WP-18), base64-encodes the audio and runs whisper,
- normalizes the text (" dot " → ".", " slash " → "/", lowercases tokens that look like hostnames) and returns `{text}`.

----
| `EVIDENCE_DIGEST_MAX` | 1.5 KB per check (this is what moves between steps and goes to the LLM) |
| `HIGH_LATENCY_MS` | median total ≥ 2000 ms or median TTFB ≥ 1500 ms |
| `LLM_TIMEOUT_MS` | 25 000 per LLM call. A timeout counts as a failure, so the fast fallback answers (*WP-07*) |
| `MONITOR_TICK_S` | 15 (on-demand), max 10 ticks |
| `BURST_TICK_S` | 60 × 15 |
| `WATCH_MIN_INTERVAL_S` | 300 |
| `WATCH_TTL` | 24 h |
| `WATCHES_PER_WS` | 3 |
| `WATCHES_GLOBAL` | 30 |
| `CRON_BATCH` | 6 |
| `RUNS_PER_HOUR` | 10 |
| `VOICE_PER_HOUR` | 20 |
| `VOICE_MAX_BYTES` | 160 KB (30 s at 24 kbps Opus) |
----

325	  /**

327	   * duplicate (no new card, the Workflow doesn't wait); a different one supersedes the pending card.
328	   */
329	  async proposeAction(runId: string, workflowId: string): Promise<Proposal> {
330	    const existing = this.sql<ApprovalRow>`SELECT * FROM approvals WHERE run_id = ${runId}`[0];
331	    if (existing) return { approvalId: existing.id, actionId: existing.action_id };
332	    const run = this.mustRun(runId);
333	    const incident = this.mustIncident();
334	    if (!run.findings_json) return { approvalId: null, actionId: null };
335	    const findings = JSON.parse(run.findings_json) as Findings;
336	    const actionId = pickAction(findings, {
337	      target: incident.target,
338	      workspaceId: incident.workspace_id,
339	      demoBaseUrl: this.env.DEMO_BASE_URL,
340	      category: findings.category,
341	      status: incident.status,
342	    });
343	    if (!actionId) return { approvalId: null, actionId: null };
344	
345	    const pending = this.sql<ApprovalRow>`SELECT * FROM approvals WHERE status = 'pending'`;
346	    if (pending.some((a) => a.action_id === actionId)) {
347	      this.event("proposal_deduped", { runId, actionId });
348	      this.syncState();
349	      return { approvalId: null, actionId };
350	    }
351	    const now = Date.now();
352	    for (const old of pending) {
353	      this.sql`UPDATE approvals SET status = 'superseded', decided_at = ${now} WHERE id = ${old.id}`;
354	      this.event("approval", { approvalId: old.id, status: "superseded" });
355	    }
356	    const approvalId = newId();
357	    this.sql`INSERT INTO approvals (id, run_id, action_id, status, workflow_id, created_at)
358	      VALUES (${approvalId}, ${runId}, ${actionId}, 'pending', ${workflowId}, ${now})`;
359	    this.event("approval", { approvalId, status: "pending", actionId });
360	    if (incident.status === "investigating") await this.transition("awaiting_approval", "action proposed");
361	    else {
362	      this.touch();
363	      this.syncState();
364	    }
365	    // After the new card is stored, so a slow call can't leave the incident without one.
366	    for (const old of pending) await this.tellWorkflow(old, false, "superseded");
367	    return { approvalId, actionId };
368	  }

370	  /** The Workflow's outcome for a card that didn't run (rejected, expired) or whose action failed. First decision wins. */
371	  async recordApprovalOutcome(approvalId: string, decision: Decision, detail?: string): Promise<void> {
372	    const a = this.mustApproval(approvalId);
373	    const now = Date.now();
374	    if (decision === "failed") {
375	      if (a.status !== "approved") return;
376	      this.setOutcome(a.id, { ok: false, text: `The action failed: ${detail ?? "unknown error"}. Nothing was changed.` });
377	    } else if (a.status === "pending" || (decision === "expired" && a.status === "approved" && !a.outcome_json)) {
378	      // An approve that raced the timeout never ran, so the card says expired.
379	      const text = decision === "expired" ? "No action taken: the approval expired after 24 hours." : "Rejected: no action taken.";
380	      this.sql`UPDATE approvals SET status = ${decision}, decided_at = ${now}, outcome_json = ${JSON.stringify({ ok: false, text })} WHERE id = ${a.id}`;
381	      this.event("approval", { approvalId: a.id, status: decision });
382	    } else return;
383	    await this.leaveAwaitingApproval(decision === "failed" ? "action failed" : `approval ${decision}`);
384	  }

401	   */
402	  async afterAction(runId: string, approvalId: string): Promise<VerifyResult> {
403	    this.mustRun(runId);
404	    const a = this.mustApproval(approvalId);
405	    const prior = a.outcome_json ? (JSON.parse(a.outcome_json) as ActionOutcome & { verified?: boolean }) : null;
406	    if (prior?.verified !== undefined) return { pass: prior.verified, text: prior.text };
407

410	    this.sql`UPDATE approvals SET outcome_json = ${JSON.stringify({ ok: v.pass, text, verified: v.pass })} WHERE id = ${a.id}`;
411	    this.event("verified", { approvalId, pass: v.pass, status: v.status, ms: v.totalMs });
412	    const incident = this.mustIncident();
413	    if (incident.status === "awaiting_approval") {
414	      if (v.pass) {
415	        await this.transition("mitigated", "verified after the action");
416	        await this.startMonitor(1);
417	      } else await this.leaveAwaitingApproval("verify failed after the action");
418	    } else this.syncState();
419	    return { pass: v.pass, text };

422	  /** One monitor tick (D11, TDD §8.4), run by the agent scheduler. Also callable over RPC, which the tests use. */
423	  async monitorTick(): Promise<void> {
424	    const m = this.sql<MonitorRow>`SELECT * FROM monitor WHERE id = 1`[0];
425	    if (!m) return;
426	    const incident = this.mustIncident();
427	    const v = await this.quickCheck();
428	    const r = tick({ ticksLeft: m.ticks_left, passes: m.consecutive_passes }, incident.status, v.pass);
429	    this.event("monitor_tick", { pass: v.pass, passes: v.pass ? m.consecutive_passes + 1 : 0, ticksLeft: m.ticks_left - 1 });
430	    if (r.monitor) {
431	      const next = await this.schedule(MONITOR_TICK_S, "monitorTick");
432	      this.sql`UPDATE monitor SET ticks_left = ${r.monitor.ticksLeft}, consecutive_passes = ${r.monitor.passes}, schedule_id = ${next.id} WHERE id = 1`;
433	    } else {
434	      this.sql`DELETE FROM monitor WHERE id = 1`;
435	      if (r.to !== "resolved") this.event("monitor_ended", { reason: "out of ticks" });
436	    }
437	    if (r.to) await this.transition(r.to, r.to === "resolved" ? "3 passing checks in a row" : v.pass ? "recovered" : "failing again");
438	    else {
439	      this.touch();
440	      this.syncState();
441	    }
442	  }

477	  async resolve(): Promise<void> {
478	    this.requireOwner();
479	    if (this.mustIncident().status === "resolved") return;
480	    await this.stopMonitor();
481	    // A card can't act on a resolved incident: pending ones end as rejected.

486	        outcome_json = ${JSON.stringify({ ok: false, text: "No action taken: the incident was resolved by hand." })} WHERE id = ${a.id}`;
487	      this.event("approval", { approvalId: a.id, status: "rejected" });

489	    await this.transition("resolved", "resolved by hand");
490	    for (const a of pending) await this.tellWorkflow(a, false, "resolved by hand");

560	  private async leaveAwaitingApproval(reason: string) {
561	    const stillPending = this.sql`SELECT 1 FROM approvals WHERE status = 'pending'`.length > 0;
562	    if (this.mustIncident().status === "awaiting_approval" && !stillPending) await this.transition("investigating", reason);
563	    else {
564	      this.touch();
565	      this.syncState();
566	    }
567	  }
568	
569	  /** Resumes or ends the card's waiting Workflow. Returns false if it couldn't be reached (logged, never thrown). */
570	  private async tellWorkflow(a: ApprovalRow, approved: boolean, reason?: string): Promise<boolean> {
571	    if (!a.workflow_id) return false;
572	    try {
573	      if (approved) await this.approveWorkflow(a.workflow_id, { metadata: { approvalId: a.id } });
574	      else await this.rejectWorkflow(a.workflow_id, { reason: reason ?? "rejected" });
575	      return true;
576	    } catch (e) {
577	      console.warn("could not reach the approval's workflow", a.workflow_id, (e as Error).message);
578	      return false;
579	    }
580	  }
581	
582	  private quickCheck(): Promise<QuickCheckResult> {
583	    const allowOrigin = devAllowOrigin(this.env);
584	    return quickCheck(this.mustIncident().target, { fetch: (input, init) => fetch(input, init), ...(allowOrigin ? { allowOrigin } : {}) });
585	  }
586	
587	  private async startMonitor(passes: number) {
588	    await this.stopMonitor();
589	    const s = await this.schedule(MONITOR_TICK_S, "monitorTick");
590	    this.sql`INSERT INTO monitor (id, kind, ticks_left, consecutive_passes, schedule_id) VALUES (1, 'post_action', ${MONITOR_MAX_TICKS}, ${passes}, ${s.id})`;
591	    this.event("monitor_started", { ticks: MONITOR_MAX_TICKS, everySec: MONITOR_TICK_S });
592	    this.syncState();
593	  }
594	
595	  private async stopMonitor() {
596	    const m = this.sql<MonitorRow>`SELECT * FROM monitor WHERE id = 1`[0];
597	    if (!m) return;
598	    if (m.schedule_id) await this.cancelSchedule(m.schedule_id);
599	    this.sql`DELETE FROM monitor WHERE id = 1`;
```

import { Sidebar } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { INCIDENTS_CHANGED, listIncidents, type IncidentRow } from "../lib/api";
import { STATUS_TEXT } from "../lib/incident";

/** The workspace's incidents. Reloads on navigation and on changes; polling and unread dots are WP-19. */
export function IncidentSidebar() {
  const [location] = useLocation();
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<IncidentRow[] | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    const load = () => void listIncidents().then((r) => live && setRows(r));
    load();
    window.addEventListener(INCIDENTS_CHANGED, load);
    return () => {
      live = false;
      window.removeEventListener(INCIDENTS_CHANGED, load);
    };
  }, [location]);

  if (rows === null) return null;
  if (rows.length === 0) return <p className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">No incidents yet.</p>;

  return (
    <Sidebar.Group>
      <Sidebar.GroupLabel>Incidents</Sidebar.GroupLabel>
      <Sidebar.Menu>
        {rows.map((r) => {
          const host = safeHost(r.target);
          return (
            <Sidebar.MenuItem key={r.id}>
              <Sidebar.MenuButton href={`/i/${r.id}`} active={location === `/i/${r.id}`} className="h-auto py-1.5">
                <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-col">
                  <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs">{host}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">{STATUS_TEXT[r.status]}</span>
                </span>
              </Sidebar.MenuButton>
            </Sidebar.MenuItem>
          );
        })}
      </Sidebar.Menu>
    </Sidebar.Group>
  );
}

function safeHost(target: string): string {
  try {
    const u = new URL(target);
    return u.pathname.length > 1 ? `${u.hostname}${u.pathname}` : u.hostname;
  } catch {
    return target;
  }
}
```

A cron job checks due watches every minute. When a check fails, `WatchWorkflow` confirms the failure (2 of 3 checks fail), opens an incident by itself and starts the diagnosis. Later passing checks resolve the incident.\n\n**Done when (TDD §17):** watching the demo and breaking it opens an incident in about 2 minutes; restoring it resolves the incident.\n\n**User decisions:**\n1. **Own demo is checked every 60 s.** Real sites stay at 300 s or more (PRD's \"at least 5 minutes\"). Only our own Worker gets the extra load.\n2. **The \"Add to watchlist\" card is a rule action for broken real (non-demo) sites.**\n   - Approving it creates a watch **linked to the current incident**, so the incident resolves itself after 3 passing checks.\n   - A broken demo always gets \"Restore demo\" first.\n\n**Already in place:**\n- D1 `watches` table + `idx_watches_due` (`migrations/0001_init.sql`).\n- Incident columns: `incident.watch_id`, `origin: \"watch\"`, and `init({origin, watchId})` (`worker/agent/IncidentAgent.ts:156`).\n- `quickCheck(url, {fetch, allowOrigin, cap})` returns `pass`, `status`, `reason` and `subrequests` (`worker/tools/quickcheck.ts`).\n- The pure monitor reducer `tick()` (`worker/agent/monitor.ts`).\n- Helpers: `isOwnDemo` (`worker/actions/catalog.ts`), `checkUrlShape` / `devAllowOrigin` (`worker/safety/ssrf.ts`), `parseTarget` (`worker/safety/target.ts`).\n- `ACTION_INFO.add_to_watchlist` is already in the prompt (`worker/diagnosis/categories.ts`).\n- `test/integration/cron.test.ts` has 6 `it.todo` cases to fill in.\n\n## Changes\n\n### Config and constants\n- **`wrangler.jsonc`:**\n  - `watchesGlobal: 30`\n  - `watchMinIntervalSec: 300`\n  - `demoWatchIntervalSec: 60`\n  - `watchTtlHours: 24`\n- **`worker/loop/budget.ts`:** add `CRON_BATCH = 6` and `CRON_SUBREQUESTS = 45`.\n\n### D1 helpers: `worker/watch/store.ts` (new)\n- **API side:**\n  - `listWatches(db, ws)`\n  - `createWatch(db, {ws, url, intervalSec, openIncidentId?})`, which returns `{ok, watch} | {ok:false, error}` and enforces:\n    - 3 per workspace and 30 globally, counting unexpired watches only\n    - `already_watched`: same workspace and same URL\n  - `deleteWatch(db, ws, id)`\n- **Cron side:**\n  - `dueWatches(db, now)`: `next_run <= now AND expires_at > now ORDER BY next_run LIMIT 6`\n  - `recordCheck(db, id, {pass, now, intervalSec})`: sets `next_run`, `last_status`, and `consecutive_passes` (`+1` or `0`)\n  - WHERE id=? AND open_incident_id IS NULL`, returning whether it claimed\n  - `releaseIncident(db, watchId, incidentId)`\n\n### Watch API: `worker/api/watches.ts` (new; route at `/api/watches` in `router.ts`)\n- **`GET`:** returns `[{id, url, intervalSec, lastStatus, consecutivePasses, nextRun, expiresAt, openIncidentId}]`.\n- **`POST {url, intervalSec?}`:**\n  - Validate: `parseTarget` (it accepts \"demo\"), then `checkUrlShape` with the dev allow-origin; a failure is 400 `invalid_url`.\n  - Interval: own demo is 60; otherwise 300 by default, clamped to 300–3600.\n  - Expiry: `expires_at = now + 24h`, and `next_run = now`, so the next cron tick checks it.\n  - Errors: 409 `already_watched`, 409 `watch_limit` (3 per workspace), 409 `watch_capacity` (30 globally).\n- **`DELETE /:id`:** 204, or 404 if the watch isn't the caller's.\n\n### Cron: `worker/watch/cron.ts` (new) + `scheduled()` in `worker/index.ts`\n`runWatchTick(env, now)`:\n1. Delete expired watches.\n2. Select the due watches.\n3. Keep one 45-subrequest counter for the whole tick (D12). For each due watch:\n   - **Budget:** stop if fewer than 12 subrequests are left; the rest wait for the next tick.\n   - **Check:** `quickCheck(url, {cap: min(10, left)})`, then charge `subrequests + 1` (the RPC or Workflow create).\n   - **Record:** call `recordCheck`.\n   - **Incident open:** call `incidentAgent(env, open_incident_id).reportWatchCheck(pass, consecutivePasses, summary)`.\n   - **Failure, no incident open:** call `WATCH_WORKFLOW.create({ id: \"watch-<id>-<epochMinute>\", params: { watchId } })`. A duplicate-ID error is caught and ignored.\n   - **Errors:** each watch has its own try/catch, so one bad watch doesn't stop the tick.\n4. Export `scheduled(controller, env, ctx)` from the default handler, plus `export { WatchWorkflow }`.\n\n### `worker/workflows/watch.ts` (new): a plain `WorkflowEntrypoint` (D3)\n1. `step.do(\"load\")`: end if the watch is gone, expired, or already has an open incident.\n2. Re-check up to twice, 30 s apart: `step.sleep(\"30 seconds\")` then `step.do(\"recheck-n\")` with `quickCheck`.\n   - The cron failure plus one failed re-check confirms the failure (2 of 3). It stops at the first failed re-check, which saves 30 s.\n   - Two passing re-checks: end, not confirmed.\n3. `step.do(\"open\")`:\n   - Make a new ID and claim the watch with `claimIncident` **before** `init`, so a second Workflow from an overlapping failure can't open a second incident.\n   - Then `agent.init({ workspaceId, target: url, origin: \"watch\", watchId })`; `init` starts the diagnosis.\n4. A comment marks where WP-19's notify step goes.\n\n### Agent (`worker/agent/IncidentAgent.ts`)\n- **`reportWatchCheck(pass, consecutivePasses, summary)`:**\n  - Writes a `watch_check` timeline event.\n  - If the post-action monitor is running, it only records the event (the monitor is faster).\n  - Otherwise it reuses `tick({ticksLeft: 2, passes: max(0, consecutivePasses - 1)}, status, pass)`:\n    - a pass while `investigating` → `mitigated`\n    - 3 passes in a row → `resolved`\n    - a fail while `mitigated` → `investigating`\n    - `awaiting_approval` is left alone (the card is still pending).\n- **`transition()`:** on `resolved` with a `watch_id`, call `releaseIncident` in D1 (logged on error).\n- **Healthy on the first check:** a watch incident whose first diagnosis says healthy stays open until watch passes resolve it. This is the existing chat-only rule; no change.\n- **`add_to_watchlist`:**\n  - `proposeAction` computes `watchable` before `pickAction`: the incident isn't watched (`watch_id` null), its URL isn't already in the workspace's watchlist, and the caps allow one more.\n  - Executing it calls `createWatch` with `openIncidentId = this.name` and sets `incident.watch_id`.\n  - `afterAction` skips the verify check for this action and writes the outcome: \"Watching every 5 minutes for 24 hours; this incident resolves itself after 3 passing checks in a row\". Then `leaveAwaitingApproval(\"watch added\")`.\n  - Check how `DiagnosisWorkflow` uses `VerifyResult` and keep its message sensible.\n\n### Action catalog (`worker/actions/catalog.ts`)\n- **`ProposeContext`:** gets `watchable: boolean`.\n- **`proposable(\"add_to_watchlist\")`:** category ≠ healthy, not own demo, and `watchable`.\n- **`pickAction`:**\n  - Rule actions for a non-healthy result are `[restore_demo_service, add_to_watchlist]`.\n  - `restore_demo_service` wins whenever it's proposable, even over the model's own picks, so a fix beats monitoring.\n  - Otherwise the model's order comes first, as before.\n- **`execute()`:** takes `{ env, workspaceId, incidentId, target }` and handles `add_to_watchlist` using the store.\n\n### UI\n- **`web/lib/api.ts`:** `listWatches`, `addWatch` (it maps the error codes to text), `deleteWatch`.\n- **`web/components/WatchlistPanel.tsx` (new, sidebar, under the incidents):**\n  - A URL `Input` + \"Watch\" `Button`, plus a \"Watch my demo\" chip that uses `session.demoUrl` (passed down from `App`).\n  - A list of rows, each with:\n    - the host/path in mono\n    - the status as text and an icon: \"Not checked yet\" / \"Passing\" / \"Failing\" / an \"Incident open\" link\n    - \"every 1 min / 5 min\" and \"expires in Nh\"\n    - a delete button with an `aria-label`\n  - Cap and validation errors show inline (`role=\"alert\"`).\n  - Polls `GET /api/watches` every 15 s while the tab is visible. When a row's `openIncidentId` changes, it calls `notifyIncidentsChanged()`, so the incident list shows the auto-opened incident without waiting for WP-19.\n- **`IncidentSidebar`:** a watch-origin incident shows an eye icon and the word \"Watch\", not colour alone.\n- **`App.tsx`:** the sidebar becomes `<IncidentSidebar/>` + `<WatchlistPanel demoUrl=…/>`.\n\n### Tests\n- **`test/integration/cron.test.ts`:** fill in the 6 todos.\n  - Seed `watches` in D1 and call the Worker's `scheduled` with `createScheduledController` + an execution context.\n  - The fake internet already serves `broken.example.com` (500) and `ok`/`*.example.com` (200).\n  - `introspectWorkflow(env.WATCH_WORKFLOW)` asserts the create, its ID, and that a duplicate is ignored.\n  - Also: `reportWatchCheck` is called for a watch with an open incident; batch limit 6; expired watches are deleted.\n- **`test/integration/watch-workflow.test.ts` (new), using `introspectWorkflow` + `disableSleeps`:**\n  - Confirmed failure → incident with `origin: \"watch\"`, `watch_id` set, and `open_incident_id` claimed.\n  - Demo stub set back to healthy before the re-checks → no incident.\n  - A watch that already has an incident → no second incident.\n- **`test/integration/watches-api.test.ts` (new):**\n  - Validation: IP literal and `localhost` refused, \"demo\" accepted.\n  - Demo interval 60, others 300+.\n  - 3 per workspace, 30 global, already watched.\n  - Delete: own watches only.\n- **Agent (in `agent.test.ts` or a new `watch-agent.test.ts`):**\n  - `reportWatchCheck` transitions: investigating → mitigated → resolved after 3; a fail reopens; awaiting_approval is untouched.\n  - Resolving releases the D1 link.\n  - `add_to_watchlist` is proposed for a broken `broken.example.com` chat incident; approve → the watch is linked and the incident goes back to investigating.\n  - It isn't proposed when already watched, for the demo, or at the caps.\n- **Unit:** `catalog.test.ts` for the new `proposable` / `pickAction` rules (restore beats a model-picked watch).\n\n### Docs and board\n- **TASKS.md:**\n  - WP-18 🔄 → ✅ with the hash; tick the checklist.\n  - Decisions-log row: the 60 s demo interval, the rule card, the early confirm, claim-before-init, the monitor taking precedence, and the WatchlistPanel poll standing in until WP-19.\n- **TDD:** fold the decisions into §5.2, §7.1, §7.2, §7.6, §8.4, §8.5 and §14.\n- **README:** take WP-18 out of \"Designed, not built\"; add a short watch description.\n- **PROMPTS.md:** log this prompt and the answers.\n- Commit and push to `dev`.\n\n## Verification\n1. `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test`, 3 runs in a row.\n2. Local end to end with `pnpm vite --port 5181`:\n   - Trigger cron with `curl http://localhost:5181/cdn-cgi/handler/scheduled`, or let it run every minute if the Vite plugin does.\n   - Steps:\n     1. \"Watch my demo\".\n     2. Set the demo to 500 errors.\n     3. The cron check fails → `WatchWorkflow` confirms in about 30 s → an incident opens in the sidebar with origin Watch.\n     4. Approve \"Restore demo\" → resolved. Separately, restoring from the demo panel → watch passes → resolved in about 3 min.\n   - Local `.dev.vars` uses the live model, so each auto-opened diagnosis costs about 140 neurons; keep it to 1–2 runs.\n3. Browser screenshots of `WatchlistPanel` in light, dark and at 390 px.\n4. Live: after the next `dev` → `main` merge, the cron trigger is deployed. Check one watch on the live demo.\n"

## Approved Plan:
# WP-18: Watch (API, panel, cron, WatchWorkflow)

## Context

WP-18 adds the watchlist (PRD §4.3, FR-40/41, TDD §8.5, D12). A user watches an endpoint. A cron job checks due watches every minute. When a check fails, `WatchWorkflow` confirms the failure (2 of 3 checks fail), opens an incident by itself and starts the diagnosis. Later passing checks resolve the incident.

**Done when (TDD §17):** watching the demo and breaking it opens an incident in about 2 minutes; restoring it resolves the incident.

**User decisions:**
1. **Own demo is checked every 60 s.** Real sites stay at 300 s or more (PRD's "at least 5 minutes"). Only our own Worker gets the extra load.
2. **The "Add to watchlist" card is a rule action for broken real (non-demo) sites.**
   - Approving it creates a watch **linked to the current incident**, so the incident resolves itself after 3 passing checks.
   - A broken demo always gets "Restore demo" first.

**Already in place:**
- D1 `watches` table + `idx_watches_due` (`migrations/0001_init.sql`).
- Incident columns: `incident.watch_id`, `origin: "watch"`, and `init({origin, watchId})` (`worker/agent/IncidentAgent.ts:156`).
- `quickCheck(url, {fetch, allowOrigin, cap})` returns `pass`, `status`, `reason` and `subrequests` (`worker/tools/quickcheck.ts`).
- The pure monitor reducer `tick()` (`worker/agent/monitor.ts`).
- Helpers: `isOwnDemo` (`worker/actions/catalog.ts`), `checkUrlShape` / `devAllowOrigin` (`worker/safety/ssrf.ts`), `parseTarget` (`worker/safety/target.ts`).
- `ACTION_INFO.add_to_watchlist` is already in the prompt (`worker/diagnosis/categories.ts`).
- `test/integration/cron.test.ts` has 6 `it.todo` cases to fill in.

## Changes

### Config and constants
- **`wrangler.jsonc`:**
  - Add workflow `{ name: "watch", binding: "WATCH_WORKFLOW", class_name: "WatchWorkflow" }`.
  - Add `"triggers": { "crons": ["* * * * *"] }`.
  - Run `pnpm cf-typegen`.
- **`worker/lib/limits.ts` (`LIMITS`):**
  - `watchesGlobal: 30`
  - `watchMinIntervalSec: 300`
  - `demoWatchIntervalSec: 60`
  - `watchTtlHours: 24`
- **`worker/loop/budget.ts`:** add `CRON_BATCH = 6` and `CRON_SUBREQUESTS = 45`.

### D1 helpers: `worker/watch/store.ts` (new)
- **API side:**
  - `listWatches(db, ws)`
  - `createWatch(db, {ws, url, intervalSec, openIncidentId?})`, which returns `{ok, watch} | {ok:false, error}` and enforces:
    - 3 per workspace and 30 globally, counting unexpired watches only
    - `already_watched`: same workspace and same URL
  - `deleteWatch(db, ws, id)`
- **Cron side:**
  - `dueWatches(db, now)`: `next_run <= now AND expires_at > now ORDER BY next_run LIMIT 6`
  - `recordCheck(db, id, {pass, now, intervalSec})`: sets `next_run`, `last_status`, and `consecutive_passes` (`+1` or `0`)
  - `deleteExpired(db, now)`
- **Incident link:**
  - `claimIncident(db, watchId, incidentId)`: `UPDATE … SET open_incident_id=? WHERE id=? AND open_incident_id IS NULL`, returning whether it claimed
  - `releaseIncident(db, watchId, incidentId)`

### Watch API: `worker/api/watches.ts` (new; route at `/api/watches` in `router.ts`)
- **`GET`:** returns `[{id, url, intervalSec, lastStatus, consecutivePasses, nextRun, expiresAt, openIncidentId}]`.
- **`POST {url, intervalSec?}`:**
  - Validate: `parseTarget` (it accepts "demo"), then `checkUrlShape` with the dev allow-origin; a failure is 400 `invalid_url`.
  - Interval: own demo is 60; otherwise 300 by default, clamped to 300–3600.
  - Expiry: `expires_at = now + 24h`, and `next_run = now`, so the next cron tick checks it.
  - Errors: 409 `already_watched`, 409 `watch_limit` (3 per workspace), 409 `watch_capacity` (30 globally).
- **`DELETE /:id`:** 204, or 404 if the watch isn't the caller's.

### Cron: `worker/watch/cron.ts` (new) + `scheduled()` in `worker/index.ts`
`runWatchTick(env, now)`:
1. Delete expired watches.
2. Select the due watches.
3. Keep one 45-subrequest counter for the whole tick (D12). For each due watch:
   - **Budget:** stop if fewer than 12 subrequests are left; the rest wait for the next tick.
   - **Check:** `quickCheck(url, {cap: min(10, left)})`, then charge `subrequests + 1` (the RPC or Workflow create).
   - **Record:** call `recordCheck`.
   - **Incident open:** call `incidentAgent(env, open_incident_id).reportWatchCheck(pass, consecutivePasses, summary)`.
   - **Failure, no incident open:** call `WATCH_WORKFLOW.create({ id: "watch-<id>-<epochMinute>", params: { watchId } })`. A duplicate-ID error is caught and ignored.
   - **Errors:** each watch has its own try/catch, so one bad watch doesn't stop the tick.
4. Export `scheduled(controller, env, ctx)` from the default handler, plus `export { WatchWorkflow }`.

### `worker/workflows/watch.ts` (new): a plain `WorkflowEntrypoint` (D3)
1. `step.do("load")`: end if the watch is gone, expired, or already has an open incident.
2. Re-check up to twice, 30 s apart: `step.sleep("30 seconds")` then `step.do("recheck-n")` with `quickCheck`.
   - The cron failure plus one failed re-check confirms the failure (2 of 3). It stops at the first failed re-check, which saves 30 s.
   - Two passing re-checks: end, not confirmed.
3. `step.do("open")`:
   - Make a new ID and claim the watch with `claimIncident` **before** `init`, so a second Workflow from an overlapping failure can't open a second incident.
   - Then `agent.init({ workspaceId, target: url, origin: "watch", watchId })`; `init` starts the diagnosis.
4. A comment marks where WP-19's notify step goes.

### Agent (`worker/agent/IncidentAgent.ts`)
- **`reportWatchCheck(pass, consecutivePasses, summary)`:**
  - Writes a `watch_check` timeline event.
  - If the post-action monitor is running, it only records the event (the monitor is faster).
  - Otherwise it reuses `tick({ticksLeft: 2, passes: max(0, consecutivePasses - 1)}, status, pass)`:
    - a pass while `investigating` → `mitigated`
    - 3 passes in a row → `resolved`
    - a fail while `mitigated` → `investigating`
    - `awaiting_approval` is left alone (the card is still pending).
- **`transition()`:** on `resolved` with a `watch_id`, call `releaseIncident` in D1 (logged on error).
- **Healthy on the first check:** a watch incident whose first diagnosis says healthy stays open until watch passes resolve it. This is the existing chat-only rule; no change.
- **`add_to_watchlist`:**
  - `proposeAction` computes `watchable` before `pickAction`: the incident isn't watched (`watch_id` null), its URL isn't already in the workspace's watchlist, and the caps allow one more.
  - Executing it calls `createWatch` with `openIncidentId = this.name` and sets `incident.watch_id`.
  - `afterAction` skips the verify check for this action and writes the outcome: "Watching every 5 minutes for 24 hours; this incident resolves itself after 3 passing checks in a row". Then `leaveAwaitingApproval("watch added")`.
  - Check how `DiagnosisWorkflow` uses `VerifyResult` and keep its message sensible.

### Action catalog (`worker/actions/catalog.ts`)
- **`ProposeContext`:** gets `watchable: boolean`.
- **`proposable("add_to_watchlist")`:** category ≠ healthy, not own demo, and `watchable`.
- **`pickAction`:**
  - Rule actions for a non-healthy result are `[restore_demo_service, add_to_watchlist]`.
  - `restore_demo_service` wins whenever it's proposable, even over the model's own picks, so a fix beats monitoring.
  - Otherwise the model's order comes first, as before.
- **`execute()`:** takes `{ env, workspaceId, incidentId, target }` and handles `add_to_watchlist` using the store.

### UI
- **`web/lib/api.ts`:** `listWatches`, `addWatch` (it maps the error codes to text), `deleteWatch`.
- **`web/components/WatchlistPanel.tsx` (new, sidebar, under the incidents):**
  - A URL `Input` + "Watch" `Button`, plus a "Watch my demo" chip that uses `session.demoUrl` (passed down from `App`).
  - A list of rows, each with:
    - the host/path in mono
    - the status as text and an icon: "Not checked yet" / "Passing" / "Failing" / an "Incident open" link
    - "every 1 min / 5 min" and "expires in Nh"
    - a delete button with an `aria-label`
  - Cap and validation errors show inline (`role="alert"`).
  - Polls `GET /api/watches` every 15 s while the tab is visible. When a row's `openIncidentId` changes, it calls `notifyIncidentsChanged()`, so the incident list shows the auto-opened incident without waiting for WP-19.
- **`IncidentSidebar`:** a watch-origin incident shows an eye icon and the word "Watch", not colour alone.
- **`App.tsx`:** the sidebar becomes `<IncidentSidebar/>` + `<WatchlistPanel demoUrl=…/>`.

### Tests
- **`test/integration/cron.test.ts`:** fill in the 6 todos.
  - Seed `watches` in D1 and call the Worker's `scheduled` with `createScheduledController` + an execution context.
  - The fake internet already serves `broken.example.com` (500) and `ok`/`*.example.com` (200).
  - `introspectWorkflow(env.WATCH_WORKFLOW)` asserts the create, its ID, and that a duplicate is ignored.
  - Also: `reportWatchCheck` is called for a watch with an open incident; batch limit 6; expired watches are deleted.
- **`test/integration/watch-workflow.test.ts` (new), using `introspectWorkflow` + `disableSleeps`:**
  - Confirmed failure → incident with `origin: "watch"`, `watch_id` set, and `open_incident_id` claimed.
  - Demo stub set back to healthy before the re-checks → no incident.
  - A watch that already has an incident → no second incident.
- **`test/integration/watches-api.test.ts` (new):**
  - Validation: IP literal and `localhost` refused, "demo" accepted.
  - Demo interval 60, others 300+.
  - 3 per workspace, 30 global, already watched.
  - Delete: own watches only.
- **Agent (in `agent.test.ts` or a new `watch-agent.test.ts`):**
  - `reportWatchCheck` transitions: investigating → mitigated → resolved after 3; a fail reopens; awaiting_approval is untouched.
  - Resolving releases the D1 link.
  - `add_to_watchlist` is proposed for a broken `broken.example.com` chat incident; approve → the watch is linked and the incident goes back to investigating.
  - It isn't proposed when already watched, for the demo, or at the caps.
- **Unit:** `catalog.test.ts` for the new `proposable` / `pickAction` rules (restore beats a model-picked watch).

### Docs and board
- **TASKS.md:**
  - WP-18 🔄 → ✅ with the hash; tick the checklist.
  - Decisions-log row: the 60 s demo interval, the rule card, the early confirm, claim-before-init, the monitor taking precedence, and the WatchlistPanel poll standing in until WP-19.
- **TDD:** fold the decisions into §5.2, §7.1, §7.2, §7.6, §8.4, §8.5 and §14.
- **README:** take WP-18 out of "Designed, not built"; add a short watch description.
- **PROMPTS.md:** log this prompt and the answers.
- Commit and push to `dev`.

## Verification
1. `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test`, 3 runs in a row.
2. Local end to end with `pnpm vite --port 5181`:
   - Trigger cron with `curl http://localhost:5181/cdn-cgi/handler/scheduled`, or let it run every minute if the Vite plugin does.
   - Steps:
     1. "Watch my demo".
     2. Set the demo to 500 errors.
     3. The cron check fails → `WatchWorkflow` confirms in about 30 s → an incident opens in the sidebar with origin Watch.
     4. Approve "Restore demo" → resolved. Separately, restoring from the demo panel → watch passes → resolved in about 3 min.
   - Local `.dev.vars` uses the live model, so each auto-opened diagnosis costs about 140 neurons; keep it to 1–2 runs.
3. Browser screenshots of `WatchlistPanel` in light, dark and at 390 px.
4. Live: after the next `dev` → `main` merge, the cron trigger is deployed. Check one watch on the live demo.

export function checkUrlShape(raw: string, allowOrigin?: string): GuardResult {
  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(raw);
  } catch {
    return fail("invalid_url", "That isn't a valid URL.");
  }
  if (sameOrigin(url, allowOrigin)) return { ok: true, url, devAllowed: true };

  if (url.protocol !== "http:" && url.protocol !== "https:") {
    return fail("scheme", `Only http and https are allowed, not ${url.protocol.replace(":", "")}.`);
  }
  if (!ALLOWED_PORTS.has(url.port)) return fail("port", `Only ports 80 and 443 are allowed, not ${url.port}.`);

/**
 * Deterministic target parser for the first chat message (D9). Order: URL, then host token, then the
 * word "demo". No safety checks here: private hosts are extracted on purpose so the SSRF guard (WP-04)
 * can refuse them with a clear message.
 */
export type TargetResult =
  | { ok: true; url: string; host: string; source: "url" | "host" | "demo" }
  | { ok: false; error: "need_target" };

const MAX_SCAN = 2000;
const SPOKEN_DOT = /([a-z0-9-])\s+dot\s+(?=[a-z0-9])/gi;
const URL_TOKEN = /\bhttps?:\/\/[^\s<>"'`]+/i;
const TRAILING_PUNCT = /[.,;:!?)\]}'"`]+$/;
// IPv4 literal | localhost | hostname with an alphabetic TLD.
const HOST_TOKEN =
  /\b((?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}|localhost|(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,63})\.?(?![a-z0-9-])/gi;
const FILE_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
  "html", "htm", "js", "mjs", "cjs", "json", "css", "php", "txt", "md", "ts", "tsx", "jsx",
  "png", "jpg", "jpeg", "gif", "svg", "ico", "yaml", "yml", "log", "xml", "csv", "sh", "py",
]);
const DEMO_WORD = /\bdemo\b/i;

export function parseTarget(message: string, demoUrl: string): TargetResult {
  const text = message.slice(0, MAX_SCAN).replace(SPOKEN_DOT, "$1.");

  const url = fromUrlToken(text);
  if (url) return url;

  const host = fromHostToken(text);
  if (host) return host;

  if (DEMO_WORD.test(text)) {
    return { ok: true, url: demoUrl, host: new URL(demoUrl).hostname, source: "demo" };
  }
  return { ok: false, error: "need_target" };
}

function fromUrlToken(text: string): TargetResult | null {
  const match = URL_TOKEN.exec(text);
  if (!match) return null;

Math.round(requested) : LIMITS.watchMinIntervalSec;\n  return Math.min(LIMITS.watchMaxIntervalSec, Math.max(LIMITS.watchMinIntervalSec, n));\n}\n\n/** The workspace's watchlist (TDD §7.1, FR-40). Quick checks run from cron, never from these routes. */\nexport const watches = new Hono<AppEnv>()\n  .get(\"/\", async (c) => c.json((await listWatches(c.env.DB, c.get(\"workspaceId\"))).map(toView)))\n  .post(\"/\", async (c) => {\n    const ws = c.get(\"workspaceId\");\n    const body = await c.req.json<{ url?: unknown; intervalSec?: unknown }>().catch(() => ({}) as { url?: unknown; intervalSec?: unknown });\n    const input = typeof body.url === \"string\" ? body.url.trim().slice(0, MAX_INPUT) : \"\";\n    const demoUrl = demoTarget(c.env.DEMO_BASE_URL, ws);\n    // Same parser as the chat: a URL, a hostname, or \"demo\".\n    const target = parseTarget(input, demoUrl ?? \"https://demo.invalid/\");\n    if (!target.ok) return c.json({ error: \"invalid_url\", message: \"Give a URL or a hostname, like example.com, or say “demo”.\" }, 400);\n    if (target.source === \"demo\" && !demoUrl) return c.json({ error: \"demo_unavailable\" }, 503);\n    // The shape check refuses IP literals, localhost and odd ports up front; cron's quickCheck re-checks DNS.\n    const shape = checkUrlShape(target.url, devAllowOrigin(c.env));\n    if (!shape.ok) return c.json({ error: \"invalid_url\", message: shape.message }, 400);\n\n    const r = await createWatch(c.env.DB, { workspaceId: ws, url: target.url, intervalSec: watchInterval(target.url, ws, c.env.DEMO_BASE_URL, body.intervalSec) });\n    if (!r.ok) return c.json({ error: r.error }, 409);\n    return c.json(toView(r.watch), 201);\n  })\n  .delete(\"/:id\", async (c) => {\n    const id = c.req.param(\"id\");\n    if (!isId(id) || !(await deleteWatch(c.env.DB, c.get(\"workspaceId\"), id))) return c.json({ error: \"not_found\" }, 404);\n    return c.body(null, 204);\n  });\n"

Due watches get a quick check (no LLM) within one subrequest\n * budget for the whole tick; whatever doesn't fit waits for the next tick. A failure with no open incident\n * starts WatchWorkflow, which confirms it before opening one. A watch with an open incident reports to it.\n */\nexport async function runWatchTick(env: Env, now: number = Date.now()): Promise<TickSummary> {\n  const out: TickSummary = { checked: 0, failed: 0, started: 0, reported: 0, expired: 0, deferred: 0 };\n  out.expired = await deleteExpired(env.DB, now);\n  const due = await dueWatches(env.DB, now);\n  const allowOrigin = devAllowOrigin(env);\n  let used = 0;\n\n  for (const [i, w] of due.entries()) {\n    if (CRON_SUBREQUESTS - used < PER_WATCH) {\n      out.deferred = due.length - i;\n      break;\n    }\n    try {\n      const v = await quickCheck(w.url, { fetch: (input, init) => fetch(input, init), cap: QUICKCHECK_CAP, ...(allowOrigin ? { allowOrigin } : {}) });\n      used += v.subrequests + 1;\n      out.checked++;\n      if (!v.pass) out.failed++;\n      const passes = await recordWatchCheck(env.DB, w, v.pass, now);\n      if (w.open_incident_id) {\n        await (await incidentAgent(env, w.open_incident_id)).reportWatchCheck(v.pass, passes, checkSummary(v));\n        out.reported++;\n      } else if (!v.pass && (await startWatchWorkflow(env, w, now))) out.started++;\n    } catch (e) {\n      // One bad watch doesn't stop the others.\n      console.error(\"watch check failed\", w.id, (e as Error).message);\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** `watch-<id>-<epochMinute>`: a second create for the same minute is a duplicate and is ignored. */\nexport const watchWorkflowId = (watchId: string, now: number) => `watch-${watchId}-${Math.floor(now / 60_000)}`;\n\nasync function startWatchWorkflow(env: Env, w: WatchRow, now: number): Promise<boolean> {\n  try {\n    await env.WATCH_WORKFLOW.create({ id: watchWorkflowId(w.id, now), params: { watchId: w.id } satisfies WatchParams });\n    return true;\n  } catch (e) {\n    if (/already exists|duplicate/i.test((e as Error).message)) return false;\n    throw e;\n  }\n}\n"
}

The incident's\n * diagnosis starts from `init`; notifying (WP-19) will be a step after \"open\".\n */\nexport class WatchWorkflow extends WorkflowEntrypoint<Env, WatchParams> {\n  override async run(event: WorkflowEvent<WatchParams>, step: WorkflowStep): Promise<WatchOutcome> {\n    const env = this.env;\n    const retries = { retries: { limit: 2, delay: \"1 second\" as const, backoff: \"exponential\" as const } };\n\n    const watch = await step.do(\"load\", retries, async () => {\n      const w = await getWatch(env.DB, event.payload.watchId);\n      if (!w || w.expires_at <= Date.now()) return null;\n      return { id: w.id, url: w.url, workspaceId: w.workspace_id, openIncidentId: w.open_incident_id };\n    });\n    if (!watch) return { confirmed: false, reason: \"gone\" };\n    if (watch.openIncidentId) return { confirmed: false, reason: \"already_open\" };\n\n    let confirmed = false;\n    for (let n = 1; n <= RECHECKS && !confirmed; n++) {\n      await step.sleep(`wait-${n}`, RECHECK_GAP);\n      const pass = await step.do(`recheck-${n}`, retries, async () => {\n        const allowOrigin = devAllowOrigin(env);\n        return (await quickCheck(watch.url, { fetch: (input, init) => fetch(input, init), ...(allowOrigin ? /**
 * The approval tail (TDD §8.2, FR-20 to FR-23): wait for the human, then run the action and verify it. Rejected
 * or expired cards are recorded, and nothing runs. A failure here never touches the finished run.
 */
async function approvalTail(io: RunIO, runId: string, approvalId: string): Promise<ActionSummary> {
  try {
    const decision = await io.waitForApproval();
    if (decision !== "approved") {
      await io.step(`approval-${decision}`, () => io.agent.recordApprovalOutcome(approvalId, decision));
      return { approvalId, decision };
    }
    let out: ActionOutcome;
    try {
      out = await io.step("action", () => io.agent.executeAction(approvalId));
    } catch (e) {
      out = { ok: false, text: (e as Error).message };
    }
    if (!out.ok) {
      const detail = out.text;
      await io.step("action-failed", () => io.agent.recordApprovalOutcome(approvalId, "failed", detail));
      return { approvalId, decision, verified: false };
    }
    const v = await io.step("verify", () => io.agent.afterAction(runId, approvalId));
    return { approvalId, decision, verified: v.pass };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("approval tail failed", runId, approvalId, (e as Error).message);
    return { approvalId, decision: "error" };
  }
}

function errorResult(evidenceId: string, tool: ToolName, e: unknown): ToolResult {
  const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
  return { evidenceId, tool, ok: false, status: "error", summary: `Check failed: ${message.slice(0, 200)}`, digest: JSON.stringify({ error: message.slice(0, 200) }), raw: null };
}

function toLine(r: ToolResult): EvidenceLine {
  return { evidenceId: r.evidenceId, tool: r.tool, status: r.status, summary: r.summary, digest: r.digest };
}

import type { ApprovalView } from "../../worker/agent/state";

export type CardState = { text: string; tone: "waiting" | "working" | "good" | "bad" | "closed" };

/** What an approval card says about itself (TDD §12.2): always in words, never colour alone. */
export function cardState(a: Pick<ApprovalView, "status" | "outcome">): CardState {
  switch (a.status) {
    case "pending":
      return { text: "Waiting for your approval", tone: "waiting" };
    case "approved":
      if (!a.outcome) return { text: "Approved, working on it…", tone: "working" };
      if (!a.outcome.ok) return a.outcome.verified === false ? { text: "Approved, but still failing", tone: "bad" } : { text: "Approved, but the action failed", tone: "bad" };
      // The action ran; the card only says "verified" once the re-check after it has passed.
      return a.outcome.verified ? { text: "Approved and verified", tone: "good" } : { text: "Approved, checking it…", tone: "working" };
    case "rejected":
      return { text: "Rejected", tone: "closed" };
    case "expired":
      return { text: "Expired, no action taken", tone: "closed" };
    case "superseded":
      return { text: "Replaced by a newer proposal", tone: "closed" };
  }
}

/** The "why" as markdown: the cause, with its evidence refs appended when the text doesn't cite them itself. */
export function whyText(a: Pick<ApprovalView, "cause" | "evidence">): string {
  if (/\[[a-z]+-\d+\]/.test(a.cause) || a.evidence.length === 0) return a.cause;
  return `${a.cause.replace(/\.?\s*$/, "")} ${a.evidence.map((id) => `[${id}]`).join(" ")}.`;
}

import { ACTION_TITLES, isOwnDemo, pickAction, proposable, type ProposeContext } from "../../worker/actions/catalog";
import { ACTION_IDS } from "../../worker/diagnosis/categories";

const WS = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef";
const DEMO = "https://incident-copilot-demo.acme.workers.dev";
const ctx = (over: Partial<ProposeContext> = {}): ProposeContext => ({
  target: `${DEMO}/w/${WS}/`,
  workspaceId: WS,
  demoBaseUrl: DEMO,
  category: "origin_5xx",
  status: "investigating",
  ...over,
});

describe("isOwnDemo", () => {
  it("matches this workspace's demo pages only", () => {
    expect(isOwnDemo(`${DEMO}/w/${WS}/`, WS, DEMO)).toBe(true);
    expect(isOwnDemo(`${DEMO}/w/${WS}/static/app.css`, WS, DEMO)).toBe(true);
    expect(isOwnDemo(`${DEMO}/w/fedcba9876543210fedcba9876543210/`, WS, DEMO)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOwnDemo(`https://shop.example.com/w/${WS}/`, WS, DEMO)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOwnDemo(`${DEMO}/`, WS, DEMO)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOwnDemo(`${DEMO}/w/${WS}/`, WS, "https://incident-copilot-demo.<sub>.workers.dev")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("proposable", () => {
  it("restore_demo_service: own broken demo, while investigating or awaiting approval", () => {
    expect(proposable("restore_demo_service", ctx())).toBe(true);
    expect(proposable("restore_demo_service", ctx({ status: "awaiting_approval", category: "inconclusive" }))).toBe(true);
    expect(proposable("restore_demo_service", ctx({ category: "healthy" }))).toBe(false);
    expect(proposable("restore_demo_service", ctx({ target: "https://shop.example.com/" }))).toBe(false);
    expect(proposable("restore_demo_service", ctx({ status: "mitigated" }))).toBe(false);
    expect(proposable("restore_demo_service", ctx({ status: "resolved" }))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("keeps the actions of later WPs switched off", () => {
    for (const a of ["add_to_watchlist", "recheck_burst", "generate_postmortem"] as const) expect(proposable(a, ctx())).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("pickAction", () => {
  it("adds the rule action when the model left it out (user decision, WP-12)", () => {
    expect(pickAction({ category: "high_latency", actions: [] }, ctx())).toBe("restore_demo_service");
    expect(pickAction({ category: "redirect_loop", actions: ["add_to_watchlist"] }, ctx())).toBe("restore_demo_service");

  });

  it("has a title for every action", () => {
    for (const a of ACTION_IDS) expect(ACTION_TITLES[a]).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

import { introspectWorkflow } from "cloudflare:test";
import { env } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { RunMode } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import { incidentAgent } from "../../worker/agent/stub";
import { newId } from "../../worker/lib/ids";

/**
 * Real DiagnosisWorkflow runs: local Workflows, the mock LLM (LLM_MODE=mock) and the fake internet from
 * vitest.config.ts (broken.example.com → 500, ok.example.com → 200, DoH for *.example.com).
 */
async function diagnoseRun(message: string, target: string, mode?: RunMode) {
  const introspector = await introspectWorkflow(env.DIAGNOSIS_WORKFLOW);
  try {
    const id = newId();
    const agent = await incidentAgent(env, id);
    const { runId } = await agent.init({ workspaceId: newId(), target, origin: "chat", message, ...(mode ? { mode } : {}) });
    expect(await agent.launchRun(runId!)).toBe(`diag-${id}-1`);
    const [instance] = await introspector.get();
    await instance!.waitForStatus("complete");
    return { id, agent, runId: runId!, output: await instance!.getOutput(), snapshot: await agent.getSnapshot() };
  } finally {
    await introspector.dispose();
  }
}

const finished = (s: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof diagnoseRun>>["snapshot"]) => s.timeline.filter((t) => t.type === "run_finished").map((t) => t.detail.status);

describe("DiagnosisWorkflow (mock LLM, fake internet)", () => {
  it("diagnoses a failing origin end to end", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s, output, runId } = await diagnoseRun("why is broken.example.com down?", "https://broken.example.com/");
    // The baseline DNS + HTTP checks run before the first AI turn (WP-13b), so one turn is enough.
    expect(output).toMatchObject({ status: "done", mode: "ai", toolCalls: 2, llmTurns: 1, category: "origin_5xx" });
    expect(s.checks.map((c) => [c.evidenceId, c.status])).toEqual([
      ["dns-1", "pass"],
      ["http-1", "fail"],
    ]);
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "origin_5xx", status: "investigating", activeRunId: null, mode: "ai" });
    const narration = s.messages.find((m) => m.id === `narr-${runId}`)!;
    expect(narration.content).toContain("[http-1]");
    expect(finished(s)).toEqual(["done"]);
  });

  it("resolves a healthy site on its first run", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s } = await diagnoseRun("is ok.example.com up?", "https://ok.example.com/");
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "healthy", status: "resolved", activeRunId: null });
  });
```

```
import { introspectWorkflow, type WorkflowIntrospector } from "cloudflare:test";
import { env } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { IncidentState } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import { incidentAgent } from "../../worker/agent/stub";
import { newId } from "../../worker/lib/ids";
import { connect } from "./helpers/ws";

/**
 * Approve → act → verify → monitor (TDD §8.2–§8.4) with real local Workflows, the mock LLM, and the demo stub
 * serving the test demo host through the fake internet (vitest.config.ts): "errors" → 500, otherwise 200.
 */
const demoStub = env.DEMO as unknown as typeof env.DEMO & { calls(ws: string): Promise<{ ws: string; modes: string[] }[]> };

let introspector: WorkflowIntrospector | null = null;
afterEach(async () => {
  await introspector?.dispose();
  introspector = null;
});

async function until(check: (s: IncidentState) => boolean, get: () => Promise<IncidentState>, ms = 8000): Promise<IncidentState> {
  const end = Date.now() + ms;
  for (;;) {
    const s = await get();
    if (check(s)) return s;
    if (Date.now() > end) throw new Error(`timed out; status=${s.status} approvals=${JSON.stringify(s.approvals.map((a) => a.status))}`);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
  }
}

/**
 * The card is shown *and* the run is finished. The Workflow proposes the card one step before `finish`, so
 * waiting for the card alone can catch the run still active (a real flake, reproduced by polling every 1 ms).
 */
const cardShown = (s: IncidentState) => s.approvals.length > 0 && s.activeRunId === null;

/** Breaks a fresh workspace's demo, asks about it, and waits for the approval card. */
async function brokenDemo(opts: { timeout?: boolean } = {}) {
  introspector = await introspectWorkflow(env.DIAGNOSIS_WORKFLOW);
  if (opts.timeout) await introspector.modifyAll((m) => m.forceEventTimeout({ name: "wait-for-approval" }));
  const ws = newId();
  const id = newId();
  await env.DEMO.setModes(ws, ["errors"]);
  const agent = await incidentAgent(env, id);
  const { runId } = await agent.init({ workspaceId: ws, target: `${env.DEMO_BASE_URL}/w/${ws}/`, origin: "chat", message: "what's wrong with my demo service?" });
  await agent.launchRun(runId!);
  const [instance] = await introspector.get();
  const snapshot = () => agent.getSnapshot();
  return { ws, id, agent, instance: instance!, snapshot };
}

describe("approval: restore the demo (FR-20 to FR-23)", () => {
  it("proposes a card, and on approve restores, verifies, and resolves after the monitor's passes", async () => {
    const { ws, agent, instance, snapshot } = await brokenDemo();
    const card = await until(cardShown, snapshot);
    expect(card).toMatchObject({ status: "awaiting_approval", category: "origin_5xx", activeRunId: null });
    expect(card.approvals[0]).toMatchObject({ actionId: "restore_demo_service", title: "Restore the demo service to healthy", status: "pending", evidence: ["http-1", "dns-1"] });
    expect(card.approvals[0]!.cause).toBeTruthy();

    expect(await agent.approve(card.approvals[0]!.id)).toEqual({ status: "approved" });
    await instance.waitForStatus("complete");
    expect(await instance.getOutput()).toMatchObject({ category: "origin_5xx", action: { decision: "approved", verified: true } });
    expect(await demoStub.calls(ws)).toEqual([
      { ws, modes: ["errors"] },
      { ws, modes: ["healthy"] },
    ]);

    await agent.monitorTick();
    expect(await snapshot()).toMatchObject({ status: "mitigated", monitor: { passes: 2, ticksLeft: 9 } });
    await agent.monitorTick();
    s = await snapshot();
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ status: "resolved", monitor: null });
    expect(s.timeline.filter((t) => t.type === "status").map((t) => t.detail.to)).toEqual(["awaiting_approval", "mitigated", "resolved"]);
  });

}

10_000 + i }));\n    const later = await seedWatch({ next_run: now + 60_000 });\n\n    const s = await runWatchTick(env, now);\n    expect(s).toMatchObject({ checked: 6, failed: 0, started: 0, deferred: 0 });\n    for (const w of due.slice(0, 6)) expect(await readWatch(w.id)).toMatchObject({ last_status: \"pass\", consecutive_passes: 1, next_run: now + 300_000 });\n    for (const w of [...due.slice(6), later]) expect((await readWatch(w.id))!.last_status).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it(\"stops before the tick's subrequest budget runs out; the rest wait for the next tick\", async () => {\n    const now = Date.now();\n    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) await seedWatch({ next_run: now - 10_000 + i });\n    // Each costs 4, and a watch needs 12 left: 20 → checks 3 (used 12), then 8 left < 12.\n    expect(await runWatchTick(env, now, 20)).toMatchObject({ checked: 3, deferred: 3 });\n  });\n\n  it(\"a failing check with no open incident starts WatchWorkflow with ID watch-<id>-<minute>\", async () => {\n    introspector = await introspectWorkflow(env.WATCH_WORKFLOW);\n    await introspector.modifyAll((m) => m.disableSleeps());\n    const now = Date.now();\n    const w = await seedWatch({ url: \"https://broken.example.com/\" });\n\n    expect(await runWatchTick(env, now)).toMatchObject({ checked: 1, failed: 1, started: 1 });\n    expect(await readWatch(w.id)).toMatchObject({ last_status: \"fail\", consecutive_passes: 0 });\n    const instance = await env.WATCH_WORKFLOW.get(watchWorkflowId(w.id, now));\n    expect(instance.id).toBe(`watch-${w.id}-${Math.floor(now / 60_000)}`);\n    const [i] = await introspector.get();\n    await i!.waitForStatus(\"complete\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"a duplicate WatchWorkflow ID is ignored, so one failure opens one incident\", async () => {\n    introspector = await introspectWorkflow(env.WATCH_WORKFLOW);\n    await introspector.modifyAll((m) => m.disableSleeps());\n    const now = Date.now();\n    const w = await seedWatch({ url: \"https://broken.example.com/\" });\n    expect((await runWatchTick(env, now)).started).toBe(1);\n    // Due again within the same minute: the same ID, so no second Workflow.\n    await env.DB.prepare(\"UPDATE watches SET next_run = ?2 WHERE id = ?1\").bind(w.id, now - 1).run();\n    expect(await runWatchTick(env, now)).toMatchObject({ failed: 1, started: 0 });\n    expect(await introspector.get()).toHaveLength(1);\n    await (await introspector.get())[0]!.waitForStatus(\"complete\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"a check on a watch with an open incident calls reportWatchCheck(pass)\", async () => {\n    const id = newId();\n    const w = await seedWatch({ open_incident_id: id });\n    const agent = await incidentAgent(env, id);\n    await agent.init({ workspaceId: w.workspace_id, target: w.url, origin: \"watch\", watchId: w.id });\n\n    expect(await runWatchTick(env)).toMatchObject({ checked: 1, reported: 1, started: 0 });\n    const s = await agent.getSnapshot();\n    expect(s.timeline.find((t) => t.type === \"watch_check\")?.detail).toMatchObject({ pass: true, passes: 1 });\n    expect(s.status).toBe(\"mitigated\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"expired watches are deleted\", async () => {\n    const now = Date.now();\n    const old = await seedWatch({ expires_at: now - 1 });\n    const live = await seedWatch({ next_run: now + 60_000 });\n    expect(await runWatchTick(env, now)).toMatchObject({ expired: 1, checked: 0 });\n    expect(await readWatch(old.id)).toBeNull();\n    expect(await readWatch(live.id)).not.toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n"
}

ws: string }> {\n  const res = await exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/session`);\n  const cookie = res.headers.getSetCookie().find((c) => c.startsWith(\"__Host-ws=\"))!.split(\";\")[0]!;\n  return { cookie, ws: cookie.split(\"=\")[1]! };\n}\n\nconst add = (cookie: string, url: string, intervalSec?: number) =>\n  exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/watches`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { Cookie: cookie, \"content-type\": \"application/json\" },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ url, ...(intervalSec ? { intervalSec } : {}) }),\n  });\nconst list = async (cookie: string) => (await exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/watches`, { headers: { Cookie: cookie } })).json<{ id: string; url: string; intervalSec: number }[]>();\nconst del = (cookie: string, id: string) => exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/watches/${id}`, { method: \"DELETE\", headers: { Cookie: cookie } });\n\ndescribe(\"/api/watches (FR-40)\", () => {\n  it(\"adds a URL or hostname, checked from the next cron tick, expiring in 24 h\", async () => {\n    const { cookie } = await workspace();\n    const before = Date.now();\n    const res = await add(cookie, \"shop.example.com\");\n    expect(res.status).toBe(201);\n    const w = await res.json<{ url: string; 3 in a row → resolved, which frees the watch\", async () => {\n    const { w, agent } = await watchIncident();\n    await agent.reportWatchCheck(true, 1, \"HTTP 200 in 40 ms\");\n    expect((await agent.getSnapshot()).status).toBe(\"mitigated\");\n    await agent.reportWatchCheck(true, 2, \"HTTP 200 in 40 ms\");\n    expect((await agent.getSnapshot()).status).toBe(\"mitigated\");\n    await agent.reportWatchCheck(true, 3, \"HTTP 200 in 40 ms\");\n    const s = await agent.getSnapshot();\n    expect(s.status).toBe(\"resolved\");\n    expect(statuses(s)).toEqual([\"mitigated\", \"resolved\"]);\n    expect((await readWatch(w.id))!.open_incident_id).toBeNull();\n    // Nothing more happens once resolved.\n    await agent.reportWatchCheck(false, 0, \"Failing: http_500\");\n    expect((await agent.getSnapshot()).status).toBe(\"resolved\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"a failure reopens a mitigated incident; a failure while investigating changes nothing\", async () => {\n    const { agent } = await watchIncident();\n    await agent.reportWatchCheck(false, 0, \"Failing: http_500\");\n    expect((await agent.getSnapshot()).status).toBe(\"investigating\");\n    await agent.reportWatchCheck(true, 1, \"HTTP 200 in 40 ms\");\n    await agent.reportWatchCheck(false, 0, \"Failing: http_500\");\n    const s = await agent.getSnapshot();\n    expect(statuses(s)).toEqual([\"mitigated\", \"investigating\"]);\n    expect(s.timeline.filter((t) => t.type === \"watch_check\")).toHaveLength(3);\n  });\n\n  it(\"leaves a pending card alone\", async () => {\n    await clearWatches();\n    const { agent, runId } = await watchIncident();\n    // The incident's own watch makes add_to_watchlist unproposable, so use a fresh chat incident for a card.\n    const chat = await incidentAgent(env, newId());\n    const r = await chat.init({ workspaceId: newId(), target: \"https://broken.example.com/\", origin: \"chat\", message: \"why?\" });\n    await chat.setFindings(r.runId!, broken);\n    expect(await chat.proposeAction(r.runId!, \"wf-test\")).toMatchObject({ actionId: \"add_to_watchlist\" });\n    await chat.reportWatchCheck(true, 1, \"HTTP 200 in 40 ms\");\n    expect((await chat.getSnapshot()).status).toBe(\"awaiting_approval\");\n    // And the watch incident itself gets no card: it's already watched.\n    await agent.setFindings(runId, broken);\n    expect(await agent.proposeAction(runId, \"wf-test\")).toEqual({ approvalId: null, actionId: null });\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"add_to_watchlist (user decision, WP-18)\", () => {\n  it(\"a broken real site gets the card; approving links a new watch, and its passes resolve the incident\", async () => {\n    await clearWatches();\n    introspector = await introspectWorkflow(env.DIAGNOSIS_WORKFLOW);\n    const ws = newId();\n    const id = newId();\n    const agent = await incidentAgent(env, id);\n    const { runId } = await agent.init({ workspaceId: ws, target: \"https://broken.example.com/\", origin: \"chat\", message: \"why is broken.example.com down?\" });\n    await agent.launchRun(runId!);\n    const [instance] = await introspector.get();\n    let s = await agent.getSnapshot();\n    for (const end = Date.now() + 8000; import { Banner, Checkbox, ClipboardText, Radio } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { ArrowSquareOutIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { DEMO_MODE_LABELS, DEMO_PROBLEMS, modesFrom, type DemoProblem } from "../../demo-service/src/modes";
import { DEMO_CHANGED, ERROR_TEXT, getDemo, setDemoModes, type Demo } from "../lib/api";

const SAVE_FAILED = "Couldn't change the mode. Try again.";
const NONE = "none";
type Choice = DemoProblem | typeof NONE;

/**
 * "Break the demo service" panel (FR-33, TDD §12.1). One main problem at a time (they change how the page
 * answers), plus an optional slow-down on top, so every combination stays visible to the diagnosis.
 */
export function DemoPanel() {
  const [demo, setDemo] = useState<Demo | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    const load = () =>
      void getDemo().then((r) => {
        if (!live) return;
        if (r.ok) {
          setDemo(r.demo);
          setError(null);
        } else setError(ERROR_TEXT[r.error]);
      });
    load();
    window.addEventListener(DEMO_CHANGED, load);
    return () => {
      live = false;
      window.removeEventListener(DEMO_CHANGED, load);
    };
  }, []);

  const problem = DEMO_PROBLEMS.find((p) => demo?.modes.includes(p)) ?? null;
  const slow = demo?.modes.includes("slow") ?? false;

  const change = async (nextProblem: DemoProblem | null, nextSlow: boolean) => {
    if (!demo) return;
    const before = demo;
    const modes = modesFrom(nextProblem, nextSlow);
    setDemo({ ...demo, modes });
    setSaving(true);
    const r = await setDemoModes(modes);
    setSaving(false);
    if (r.ok) {
      setDemo(r.demo);
      setError(null);
    } else {
      setDemo(before);
      setError(r.error === "demo_unavailable" ? ERROR_TEXT.demo_unavailable : SAVE_FAILED);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="demo-panel-title" className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div>
        <h2 id="demo-panel-title" className="font-display text-base font-medium">
          Demo service
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Break your own demo site, then ask the copilot what's wrong.</p>
      </div>

      {demo && (
        <>
          <p className="text-sm" aria-live="polite">
            <span className="text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Now: </span>
            <span className="font-medium">{demo.modes.map((m) => DEMO_MODE_LABELS[m]).join(" + ")}</span>
          </p>

          <Radio.Group<Choice>
            legend="Problem"
            value={problem ?? NONE}
            onValueChange={(c) => void change(c === NONE ? null : c, slow)}
            disabled={saving}
          >
            <Radio.Item<Choice> value={NONE} label="None" />
            {DEMO_PROBLEMS.map((p) => (
              <Radio.Item<Choice> key={p} value={p} label={DEMO_MODE_LABELS[p]} />
            ))}
          </Radio.Group>

          <Checkbox label="Also slow (+3 s per response)" checked={slow} disabled={saving} onCheckedChange={(c) => void change(problem, c === true)} />

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Demo URL</span>
            <ClipboardText text={demo.url} className="w-full min-w-0" tooltip={{ text: "Copy URL", copiedText: "Copied" }} />
            <a
              href={demo.url}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noreferrer"
              className="flex w-fit items-center gap-1 rounded-md text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted) hover:text-(--cf-fg) focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cf-orange"
            >
              Open the demo
              <ArrowSquareOutIcon aria-hidden size={14} />
            </a>
          </div>

          <p className="text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
            Then ask: <q className="text-(--cf-fg)">What's wrong with my demo service?</q>
          </p>
        </>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div role="alert">
          <Banner variant="error" description={error} />
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

- `shape`: enum [default: base]
  - `"base"`: Default rectangular button shape
  - `"square"`: Square button for icon-only actions
  - `"circle"`: Circular button for icon-only actions
- `size`: enum [default: base]
  - `"xs"`: Extra small button for compact UIs
  - `"sm"`: Small button for secondary actions
--
- `variant`: enum [default: secondary]
  - `"primary"`: High-emphasis button for primary actions
  - `"secondary"`: Default button style for most actions
  - `"ghost"`: Minimal button with no background
  - `"destructive"`: Danger button for destructive actions like delete
  - `"secondary-destructive"`: Secondary button with destructive text for less prominent dangerous actions
  - `"outline"`: Bordered button with transparent background

Cron checks them; when a\n * failure is confirmed an incident opens by itself, and this panel tells the incident list to reload.\n */\nexport function WatchlistPanel({ demoUrl }: { demoUrl?: string }) {\n  const [watches, setWatches] = useState<Watch[] | null>(null);\n  const [url, setUrl] = useState(\"\");\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n  const openIds = useRef<string | null>(null);\n\n  const load = async () => {\n    const w = await listWatches();\n    if (!w) return;\n    setWatches(w);\n    // A watch that just opened (or closed) an incident: the sidebar's list should show it now (polling is WP-19).\n    const ids = w.map((x) => x.openIncidentId ?? \"\").join(\",\");\n    if (openIds.current !== null && openIds.current !== ids) notifyIncidentsChanged();\n    openIds.current = ids;\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    void load();\n    const timer = setInterval(() => document.visibilityState === \"visible\" && void load(), POLL_MS);\n    const onVisible = () => document.visibilityState === \"visible\" && void load();\n    document.addEventListener(\"visibilitychange\", onVisible);\n    return () => {\n      clearInterval(timer);\n      document.removeEventListener(\"visibilitychange\", onVisible);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  const add = async (target: string) => {\n    if (!target.trim() || busy) return;\n    setBusy(true);\n    setError(null);\n    const r = await addWatch(target.trim());\n    setBusy(false);\n    if (!r.ok) return setError(r.message ?? WATCH_ERROR_TEXT[r.error]);\n    setUrl(\"\");\n    await load();\n  };\n\n  const remove = async (w: Watch) => {\n    setError(null);\n    if (!(await deleteWatch(w.id))) return setError(\"Couldn't remove that watch. ````
164:| 18 | Watchlist: a cron every minute checks due watches; on failure, `WatchWorkflow` confirms 2 of 3 and opens an incident on its own | TDD §8.5 |
  W -- "init / snapshot" --> A
  W --> D1[("D1<br/>incident index")]
  A -- "runWorkflow · approve / reject" --> F["DiagnosisWorkflow<br/>AgentWorkflow"]
  F -- "RPC: record checks, findings, narrate" --> A
  F --> AI["Workers AI<br/>via AI Gateway"]
  F --> T["Tools<br/>dns_lookup · http_probe"]
  T --> G{"SSRF guard<br/>+ origin pin"}
  G --> NET["Public internet"]
  G --> DEMO["Demo Worker<br/>/w/:workspace/"]
  A -- "Service Binding RPC<br/>restore_demo_service" --> DEMO
  A -- "narrate: streamed tokens" --> AI
```

**One request, start to finish:**
1. `POST /api/incidents` parses the target from the message (a URL, then a hostname, then the word "demo"; no LLM involved), creates an `IncidentAgent` and calls `init`.
2. The agent records the question and starts a `DiagnosisWorkflow` with the ID `diag-<incident>-<run>`.
3. The Workflow runs the baseline checks (DNS + HTTP) right away, so the first card shows in about a second. It then gives the model the evidence digests and lets it either call more tools or call `submit_findings` (category, confidence, evidence IDs, actions), which is validated with zod.
4. The agent streams a narration of those fixed findings over the WebSocket. A citation check removes any sentence that cites an unknown ID, or makes a factual claim without a citation.
5. If an action is proposable, an approval card appears and the Workflow waits for it. On approve, the agent runs the action and re-checks it. It then checks again every 15 s, and resolves the incident once 3 checks in a row have passed (the re-check plus two more).

## How the Agents SDK is used
````

There's nothing to re-check, so `afterAction` skips the verify check, stores `outcome.done`, and the incident goes back to `investigating`; the card says \"Approved and done\".'''),\n('''5.
